      - generic [ref=e169]:
        - heading "section/Introduction Introduction" [level=1] [ref=e172]:
          - link "section/Introduction" [ref=e173] [cursor=pointer]:
            - /url: "#section/Introduction"
          - text: Introduction
        - generic [ref=e175]:
          - paragraph [ref=e176]: Welcome to Api-Football! You can use our API to access all API endpoints, which can get information about Football Leagues & Cups.
          - paragraph [ref=e177]: We have language bindings in C, C#, cURL, Dart, Go, Java, Javascript, NodeJs, Objective-c, OCaml, Php, PowerShell, Python, Ruby, Shell and Swift! You can view code examples in the dark area to the right, and you can switch the programming language of the examples with the tabs in the top right.
          - paragraph [ref=e178]: The update frequency indicated in the documentation is given as an indication and may vary for certain competitions.
      - generic [ref=e179]:
        - heading "section/Authentication Authentication" [level=1] [ref=e182]:
          - link "section/Authentication" [ref=e183] [cursor=pointer]:
            - /url: "#section/Authentication"
          - text: Authentication
        - generic [ref=e185]:
          - paragraph [ref=e186]:
            - text: We uses API keys to allow access to the API. You can register a new API key in our
            - link "dashboard" [ref=e187] [cursor=pointer]:
              - /url: https://dashboard.api-football.com/register
            - text: .
          - paragraph [ref=e188]:
            - strong [ref=e189]: API-SPORTS
            - text: ":"
            - link "https://v3.football.api-sports.io/" [ref=e190] [cursor=pointer]:
              - /url: https://v3.football.api-sports.io/
          - paragraph [ref=e191]: "Our API expects for the API key to be included in all API requests to the server in a header that looks like the following:"
          - blockquote [ref=e192]:
            - paragraph [ref=e193]:
              - text: Make sure to replace
              - code [ref=e194]: XxXxXxXxXxXxXxXxXxXxXxXx
              - text: with your API key.
          - paragraph [ref=e195]:
            - strong [ref=e196]: REQUESTS HEADERS & CORS
          - paragraph [ref=e197]:
            - text: The API is configured to work only with
            - strong [ref=e198]: GET
            - text: "requests and allows only the headers listed below:"
          - list [ref=e199]:
            - listitem [ref=e200]:
              - code [ref=e201]: x-apisports-key
          - paragraph [ref=e202]: If you make non-GET requests or add headers that are not in the list, you will receive an error from the API.
          - paragraph [ref=e203]:
            - text: Some frameworks
            - emphasis [ref=e204]: (especially in JS, nodeJS..)
            - text: automatically add extra headers, you have to make sure to remove them in order to get a response from the API.
      - generic [ref=e205]:
        - heading "section/Authentication/API-SPORTS-Account API-SPORTS Account" [level=2] [ref=e208]:
          - link "section/Authentication/API-SPORTS-Account" [ref=e209] [cursor=pointer]:
            - /url: "#section/Authentication/API-SPORTS-Account"
          - text: API-SPORTS Account
        - generic [ref=e211]:
          - paragraph [ref=e212]:
            - text: "If you decided to subscribe directly on our site, you have a dashboard at your disposal at the following url:"
            - link "dashboard" [ref=e213] [cursor=pointer]:
              - /url: https://dashboard.api-football.com/register
          - paragraph [ref=e214]: "It allows you to:"
          - list [ref=e215]:
            - listitem [ref=e216]: To follow your consumption in real time
            - listitem [ref=e217]: Manage your subscription and change it if necessary
            - listitem [ref=e218]: Check the status of our servers
            - listitem [ref=e219]: Test all endpoints without writing a line of code.
          - paragraph [ref=e220]:
            - text: You can also consult all this information directly through the API by calling the endpoint
            - code [ref=e221]: status
            - text: .
          - blockquote [ref=e222]:
            - paragraph [ref=e223]: This call does not count against the daily quota.
          - code [ref=e225]: "get(\"https://v3.football.api-sports.io/status\"); // response { \"get\": \"status\", \"parameters\": [], \"errors\": [], \"results\": 1, \"response\": { \"account\": { \"firstname\": \"xxxx\", \"lastname\": \"XXXXXX\", \"email\": \"xxx@xxx.com\" }, \"subscription\": { \"plan\": \"Free\", \"end\": \"2020-04-10T23:24:27+00:00\", \"active\": true }, \"requests\": { \"current\": 12, \"limit_day\": 100 } } }"
          - heading "Headers sent as response" [level=3] [ref=e226]
          - paragraph [ref=e227]: "When consuming our API, you will always receive the following headers appended to the response:"
          - list [ref=e228]:
            - listitem [ref=e229]:
              - code [ref=e230]: x-ratelimit-requests-limit
              - text: ": The number of requests allocated per day according to your subscription."
            - listitem [ref=e231]:
              - code [ref=e232]: x-ratelimit-requests-remaining
              - text: ": The number of remaining requests per day according to your subscription."
            - listitem [ref=e233]:
              - code [ref=e234]: X-RateLimit-Limit
              - text: ": Maximum number of API calls per minute."
            - listitem [ref=e235]:
              - code [ref=e236]: X-RateLimit-Remaining
              - text: ": Number of API calls remaining before reaching the limit per minute."
          - heading "Rate Limiting Policy" [level=3] [ref=e237]
          - paragraph [ref=e238]: If you exceed your allowed request rate per minute, either through continuous excessive usage or by generating abnormal traffic spikes, your access may be temporarily or permanently blocked by our firewall without prior notice. This ensures service stability and fair usage for all customers.
          - heading "Dashboard" [level=3] [ref=e239]
          - paragraph [ref=e240]:
            - img "dashboard" [ref=e241]
          - heading "Requests" [level=3] [ref=e242]
          - paragraph [ref=e243]:
            - img "requests" [ref=e244]
          - heading "Live tester" [level=3] [ref=e245]
          - paragraph [ref=e246]:
            - img "requests" [ref=e247]

      - generic [ref=e257]:
        - heading "section/Logos-Images Logos / Images" [level=1] [ref=e260]:
          - link "section/Logos-Images" [ref=e261] [cursor=pointer]:
            - /url: "#section/Logos-Images"
          - text: Logos / Images
        - generic [ref=e263]:
          - paragraph [ref=e264]:
            - text: Calls to logos/images do not count towards your daily quota and are provided for free. However these calls are subject to a
            - strong [ref=e265]: rate per second & minute
            - text: ", it is recommended to save this data on your side in order not to slow down or impact the user experience of your application or website. For this you can use"
            - strong [ref=e266]: CDNs
            - text: such as
            - link "bunny.net" [ref=e267] [cursor=pointer]:
              - /url: https://bunny.net?ref=8r6al7jhm4
            - text: .
          - paragraph [ref=e268]:
            - text: We have a tutorial available
            - link "here" [ref=e269] [cursor=pointer]:
              - /url: https://www.api-football.com/news/post/optimizing-sports-websites-bunnycdn-api-sports-image-storage-guide
            - text: ", which explains how to set up your own media system with"
            - strong [ref=e270]: BunnyCDN
            - text: .
          - paragraph [ref=e271]: Logos, images and trademarks delivered through the API are provided solely for identification and descriptive purposes (e.g., identifying leagues, teams, players or venues). We does not own any of these visual assets, and no intellectual property rights are claimed over them. Some images or data may be subject to intellectual property or trademark rights held by third parties (including but not limited to leagues, federations, or clubs). The use of such content in your applications, websites, or products may require additional authorization or licensing from the respective rights holders. You are fully responsible for ensuring that your usage of any logos, images, or branded content complies with applicable laws in your country or the countries where your services are made available. We are not affiliated with, sponsored by, or endorsed by any sports league, federation, or brand featured in the data provided.

            - text: is a Content Delivery Network

            - text: that delivers a global content distribution experience. With strategically positioned servers, BunnyCDN ensures swift and reliable delivery of static content, optimizing website performance with features like intelligent image optimization, sophisticated caching, and advanced security measures.
          - paragraph [ref=e669]:
            - strong [ref=e670]: "Unlocking Media Delivery Excellence with BunnyCDN:"
          - list [ref=e671]:
            - listitem [ref=e672]:
              - strong [ref=e673]: "Quick Configuration:"
              - text: Set up your media CDN in just 5 minutes. Define cache times, customize your domain – it's that simple.
            - listitem [ref=e674]:
              - strong [ref=e675]: "Global Accessibility:"
              - text: Leverage BunnyCDN's expansive server network for swift and dependable content delivery worldwide.

              - strong [ref=e677]: "Customized Configuration:"
              - text: Tailor caching, define cache times, and implement CORS headers to create an efficient and seamless user experience.

              - strong [ref=e679]: "Own Your Domain:"
              - text: Personalize your media delivery with your domain, enhancing your brand's online presence.
            - listitem [ref=e680]:
              - strong [ref=e681]: "Robust Security:"
              - text: BunnyCDN integrates advanced security features, guaranteeing a secure environment for delivering your content.
            - listitem [ref=e682]:
              - strong [ref=e683]: "Responsive Performance:"
              - text: Experience responsive performance without the need for prior media downloads. Discover the capabilities of BunnyCDN for optimized media delivery.

            - strong [ref=e739]: Why Choose Firebase's Realtime Database?
          - list [ref=e740]:
            - listitem [ref=e741]:
              - strong [ref=e742]: "Real-Time Data:"
              - text: Firebase allows you to store real-time data, meaning that updates are instantly propagated to all connected users.
            - listitem [ref=e743]:
              - strong [ref=e744]: "Easy Synchronization:"
              - text: Data is automatically synchronized across all devices, providing a consistent and real-time user experience.
            - listitem [ref=e745]:
              - strong [ref=e746]: "Built-In Security:"
              - text: Firebase offers flexible security rules to control data access and ensure privacy.
            - listitem [ref=e747]:
              - strong [ref=e748]: "Simplified Integration:"
              - text: Firebase's Realtime Database easily integrates with other Firebase services, simplifying backend management.
          - paragraph [ref=e749]:
            - strong [ref=e750]: "Helpful Links:"
          - list [ref=e751]:
            - listitem [ref=e752]:
              - link "Explore Firebase's Realtime Database" [ref=e753] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: https://firebase.google.com/products/realtime-database/
              - text: ": Discover the features and advantages of Firebase's Realtime Database for efficient real-time data management."
            - listitem [ref=e754]:
              - link "Firebase's Realtime Database Documentation" [ref=e755] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: https://firebase.google.com/docs/database/
              - text: ": Refer to the comprehensive documentation for Firebase's Realtime Database for a smooth integration."
          - paragraph [ref=e756]:
            - text: A tutorial describing each step is available on our blog
            - link "here" [ref=e757] [cursor=pointer]:
              - /url: https://www.api-football.com/news/post/how-to-use-firebase-with-api-football-real-time-data-integration
            - text: .
      - generic [ref=e758]:
        - heading "tag/Widgets Widgets" [level=1] [ref=e761]:
          - link "tag/Widgets" [ref=e762] [cursor=pointer]:
            - /url: "#tag/Widgets"
          - text: Widgets
        - generic [ref=e764]:
          - paragraph [ref=e765]:
            - text: API-SPORTS widgets allow you to easily display
            - strong [ref=e766]: dynamic sports data
            - text: on your website.
          - paragraph [ref=e767]: "They are designed to be:"
          - list [ref=e768]:
            - listitem [ref=e769]:
              - strong [ref=e770]: Ultra-modular
              - text: ": each component is autonomous"
            - listitem [ref=e771]:
              - strong [ref=e772]: Customisable
              - text: ": language, theme, content, behaviour"
            - listitem [ref=e773]:
              - strong [ref=e774]: Easy to integrate
              - text: ": no framework required, a simple HTML tag is all you need"
          - paragraph [ref=e775]:
            - text: They use request from your API-SPORTS account and work with
            - strong [ref=e776]: all plans
            - text: ", including the free plan."
          - paragraph [ref=e777]:
            - text: Find all the documentation on widgets
            - link "here" [ref=e778] [cursor=pointer]:
              - /url: https://api-sports.io/documentation/widgets/v3
          - img [ref=e779]
      - generic [ref=e780]:
        - heading "tag/Widgets/Security Security" [level=2] [ref=e783]:
          - link "tag/Widgets/Security" [ref=e784] [cursor=pointer]:
            - /url: "#tag/Widgets/Security"
          - text: Security
        - generic [ref=e786]:
          - paragraph [ref=e787]:
            - text: Our widgets use your account's API-KEY, which must be specified in the
            - code [ref=e788]: data-key
            - text: attribute of your widget configuration.
          - paragraph [ref=e789]:
            - text: When using these widgets it is important to be aware that your API-KEY will be visible to the users of your site, it is possible to protect yourself from this by allowing only the desired domains or IP in our
            - link "dashboard" [ref=e790] [cursor=pointer]:
              - /url: https://dashboard.api-football.com/profile?access
            - text: . This way no one else can use your API-KEY for you. If you have already set up your widget and have not activated this option, you can reset your API-KEY and activate this option after.
          - paragraph [ref=e791]:
            - text: You can further enhance security by completely hiding your API-KEY from the source code by following this
            - link "tutorial" [ref=e792] [cursor=pointer]:
              - /url: https://www.api-football.com/news/post/how-to-optimize-widgets-cache-and-security-tutorial
            - text: .
      - generic [ref=e793]:
        - heading "tag/Widgets/Caching-Data Caching Data" [level=2] [ref=e796]:
          - link "tag/Widgets/Caching-Data" [ref=e797] [cursor=pointer]:
            - /url: "#tag/Widgets/Caching-Data"
          - text: Caching Data
        - generic [ref=e799]:
          - paragraph [ref=e800]: By using Widgets, each visit to a page on your website triggers one or more API requests to retrieve data. Without a caching system, your daily quota can be reached very quickly.
          - paragraph [ref=e801]:
            - strong [ref=e802]: "Example:"
            - text: If a page triggers a single API request per visitor and you receive 80 visits to that page in one minute, this results in
            - strong [ref=e803]: 80 API requests
            - text: . Over a full day, that can add up to
            - strong [ref=e804]: 115 200 requests
            - text: .
          - paragraph [ref=e805]:
            - text: By implementing a caching system, even with a very short duration, such as
            - strong [ref=e806]: 60 seconds
            - text: ", you can drastically reduce the number of requests. The first visit will trigger an API request, but the response will then be cached for the next 60 seconds. This means that if 80 visitors access the same page within that time frame,"
            - strong [ref=e807]: only the first request
            - text: will reach the API, while the next 79 will be served directly from the cache.
          - paragraph [ref=e808]:
            - text: With this system in place, you reduce usage from
            - strong [ref=e809]: 115 200
            - text: requests per day to just
            - strong [ref=e810]: 1 440
            - text: .
          - paragraph [ref=e811]:
            - text: A full tutorial is available
            - link "here" [ref=e812] [cursor=pointer]:
              - /url: https://www.api-football.com/news/post/how-to-optimize-widgets-cache-and-security-tutorial
            - text: ", explaining step by step how to set up an effective caching system."
      - generic [ref=e813]:
        - heading "tag/Widgets/Debugging Debugging" [level=2] [ref=e816]:
          - link "tag/Widgets/Debugging" [ref=e817] [cursor=pointer]:
            - /url: "#tag/Widgets/Debugging"
          - text: Debugging
        - generic [ref=e819]:
          - paragraph [ref=e820]:
            - text: If the widget does not display the requested information, it is possible to set the
            - code [ref=e821]: data-show-errors
            - text: tag to
            - strong [ref=e822]: "true"
            - text: "to display error messages directly in the widget and in the console. This can be due to several things like :"
            - emphasis [ref=e823]: (Non-exhaustive list)
          - list [ref=e824]:
            - listitem [ref=e825]: You have reached your daily number of requests
            - listitem [ref=e826]: Tags are incorrectly filled in
            - listitem [ref=e827]: Your API-KEY is incorrect

      - heading "tag/Leagues Leagues" [level=1] [ref=e1423]:
        - link "tag/Leagues" [ref=e1424] [cursor=pointer]:
          - /url: "#tag/Leagues"
        - text: Leagues
      - generic [ref=e1426]:
        - generic [ref=e1427]:
          - heading "tag/Leagues/operation/get-leagues Leagues" [level=2] [ref=e1428]:
            - link "tag/Leagues/operation/get-leagues" [ref=e1429] [cursor=pointer]:
              - /url: "#tag/Leagues/operation/get-leagues"
            - text: Leagues
          - generic [ref=e1431]:
            - paragraph [ref=e1432]: Get the list of available leagues and cups.
            - paragraph [ref=e1433]:
              - text: The league
              - code [ref=e1434]: id
              - text: are
              - strong [ref=e1435]: unique
              - text: in the API and leagues keep it across all
              - code [ref=e1436]: seasons
            - paragraph [ref=e1437]:
              - text: "To get the logo of a competition you have to call the following url:"
              - code [ref=e1438]: "https://media.api-sports.io/football/leagues/{league_id}.png"
            - paragraph [ref=e1439]:
              - text: This endpoint also returns the
              - code [ref=e1440]: coverage
              - text: of each competition, which makes it possible to know what is available for that league or cup.
            - paragraph [ref=e1441]:
              - text: The values returned by the coverage indicate the
              - strong [ref=e1442]: data available at the moment
              - text: you call the API, so for a competition that has not yet started, it is normal to have all the features set to
              - code [ref=e1443]: "False"
              - text: . This will be updated once the competition has started.
            - blockquote [ref=e1444]:
              - paragraph [ref=e1445]:
                - text: You can find all the leagues ids on our
                - link "Dashboard" [ref=e1446] [cursor=pointer]:
                  - /url: https://dashboard.api-football.com/soccer/ids
                - text: .
            - paragraph [ref=e1447]:
              - strong [ref=e1448]: "Example :"
            - code [ref=e1450]: "\"coverage\": { \"fixtures\": { \"events\": true, \"lineups\": true, \"statistics_fixtures\": false, \"statistics_players\": false }, \"standings\": true, \"players\": true, \"top_scorers\": true, \"top_assists\": true, \"top_cards\": true, \"injuries\": true, \"predictions\": true, \"odds\": false }"
            - paragraph [ref=e1451]:
              - text: "In this example we can deduce that the competition does not have the following features:"
              - code [ref=e1452]: statistics_fixtures
              - text: ","
              - code [ref=e1453]: statistics_players
              - text: ","
              - code [ref=e1454]: odds
              - text: because it is set to
              - code [ref=e1455]: "False"
              - text: .
            - paragraph [ref=e1456]:
              - text: The coverage of a competition can vary from season to season and values set to
              - code [ref=e1457]: "True"
              - text: do not guarantee 100% data availability.
            - paragraph [ref=e1458]:
              - text: Some competitions, such as the
              - code [ref=e1459]: friendlies
              - text: ", are exceptions to the coverage indicated in the"
              - code [ref=e1460]: leagues
              - text: endpoint, and the data available may differ depending on the match, including livescore, events, lineups, statistics and players.
            - paragraph [ref=e1461]: Competitions are automatically renewed by the API when a new season is available. There may be a delay between the announcement of the official calendar and the availability of data in the API.
            - paragraph [ref=e1462]:
              - text: For
              - code [ref=e1463]: Cup
              - text: competitions, fixtures are automatically added when the two participating teams are known. For example if the current phase is the 8th final, the quarter final will be added once the teams playing this phase are known.
            - blockquote [ref=e1464]:
              - paragraph [ref=e1465]: Examples available in Request samples "Use Cases".
            - blockquote [ref=e1466]:
              - paragraph [ref=e1467]: Most of the parameters of this endpoint can be used together.
            - paragraph [ref=e1468]:
              - strong [ref=e1469]: Update Frequency
              - text: ": This endpoint is updated several times a day."
            - paragraph [ref=e1470]:
              - strong [ref=e1471]: Recommended Calls
              - text: ": 1 call per hour."

                  - generic [ref=e1646]: "// Allows to retrieve all the seasons available for a league/cup get(\"https://v3.football.api-sports.io/leagues?id=39\"); // Get all leagues from one league {name} get(\"https://v3.football.api-sports.io/leagues?name=premier league\"); // Get all leagues from one {country} // You can find the available {country} by using the endpoint country get(\"https://v3.football.api-sports.io/leagues?country=england\"); // Get all leagues from one country {code} (GB, FR, IT etc..) // You can find the available country {code} by using the endpoint country get(\"https://v3.football.api-sports.io/leagues?code=gb\"); // Get all leagues from one {season} // You can find the available {season} by using the endpoint seasons get(\"https://v3.football.api-sports.io/leagues?season=2019\"); // Get one league from one league {id} & {season} get(\"https://v3.football.api-sports.io/leagues?season=2019&id=39\"); // Get all leagues in which the {team} has played at least one match get(\"https://v3.football.api-sports.io/leagues?team=33\"); // Allows you to search for a league in relation to a league {name} or {country} get(\"https://v3.football.api-sports.io/leagues?search=premier league\"); get(\"https://v3.football.api-sports.io/leagues?search=England\"); // Get all leagues from one {type} get(\"https://v3.football.api-sports.io/leagues?type=league\"); // Get all leagues where the season is in progress or not get(\"https://v3.football.api-sports.io/leagues?current=true\"); // Get the last 99 leagues or cups added to the API get(\"https://v3.football.api-sports.io/leagues?last=99\"); // It’s possible to make requests by mixing the available parameters get(\"https://v3.football.api-sports.io/leagues?season=2019&country=england&type=league\"); get(\"https://v3.football.api-sports.io/leagues?team=85&season=2019\"); get(\"https://v3.football.api-sports.io/leagues?id=61¤t=true&type=league\");"

            - link "tag/Teams/operation/get-teams-statistics" [ref=e2099] [cursor=pointer]:
              - /url: "#tag/Teams/operation/get-teams-statistics"
            - text: Teams statistics
          - generic [ref=e2101]:
            - paragraph [ref=e2102]: Returns the statistics of a team in relation to a given competition and season.
            - paragraph [ref=e2103]:
              - text: It is possible to add the
              - code [ref=e2104]: date
              - text: parameter to calculate statistics from the beginning of the season to the given date. By default the API returns the statistics of all games played by the team for the competition and the season.
            - paragraph [ref=e2105]:
              - strong [ref=e2106]: Update Frequency
              - text: ": This endpoint is updated twice a day."
            - paragraph [ref=e2107]:
              - strong [ref=e2108]: Recommended Calls
              - text: ": 1 call per day for the teams who have at least one fixture during the day otherwise 1 call per week."
            - blockquote [ref=e2109]:
              - paragraph [ref=e2110]: Here is an example of what can be achieved
            - paragraph [ref=e2111]:
              - img "demo-teams-statistics" [ref=e2112]

      - generic [ref=e3286]:
        - generic [ref=e3287]:
          - heading "tag/Fixtures/operation/get-fixtures Fixtures" [level=2] [ref=e3288]:
            - link "tag/Fixtures/operation/get-fixtures" [ref=e3289] [cursor=pointer]:
              - /url: "#tag/Fixtures/operation/get-fixtures"
            - text: Fixtures
          - generic [ref=e3291]:
            - paragraph [ref=e3292]:
              - text: For all requests to fixtures you can add the query parameter
              - code [ref=e3293]: timezone
              - text: to your request in order to retrieve the list of matches in the time zone of your choice like
              - emphasis [ref=e3294]: “Europe/London“
            - paragraph [ref=e3295]: To know the list of available time zones you have to use the endpoint timezone.
            - paragraph [ref=e3296]:
              - strong [ref=e3297]: Available fixtures status

            - paragraph [ref=e3401]:
              - text: Fixtures with the status
              - code [ref=e3402]: TBD
              - text: may indicate an incorrect fixture date or time because the fixture date or time is not yet known or final. Fixtures with this status are checked and updated daily. The same applies to fixtures with the status
              - code [ref=e3403]: PST
              - text: ","
              - code [ref=e3404]: CANC
              - text: .
            - paragraph [ref=e3405]:
              - text: The fixtures ids are unique and specific to each fixture. In no case an
              - code [ref=e3406]: ID
              - text: will change.
            - paragraph [ref=e3407]:
              - text: Not all competitions have livescore available and only have
              - code [ref=e3408]: final result
              - text: . In this case, the status remains in
              - code [ref=e3409]: NS
              - text: and will be updated in the minutes/hours following the match (this can take up to 48 hours, depending on the competition).
            - blockquote [ref=e3410]:
              - paragraph [ref=e3411]: Although the data is updated every 15 seconds, depending on the competition there may be a delay between reality and the availability of data in the API.
            - paragraph [ref=e3412]:
              - strong [ref=e3413]: Update Frequency
              - text: ": This endpoint is updated every 15 seconds."
            - paragraph [ref=e3414]:
              - strong [ref=e3415]: Recommended Calls
              - text: ": 1 call per minute for the leagues, teams, fixtures who have at least one fixture in progress otherwise 1 call per day."
            - blockquote [ref=e3416]:
              - paragraph [ref=e3417]: Here are several examples of what can be achieved
            - paragraph [ref=e3418]:
              - img "demo-fixtures" [ref=e3419]

// Get all available fixtures in play // In this request events are returned in the response get(\"https://v3.football.api-sports.io/fixtures?live=all\"); // Get all available fixtures in play filter by several {league} // In this request events are returned in the response get(\"https://v3.football.api-sports.io/fixtures?live=39-61-48\"); // Get all available fixtures from one {league} & {season} get(\"https://v3.football.api-sports.io/fixtures?league=39&season=2019\"); // Get all available fixtures from one {date} get(\"https://v3.football.api-sports.io/fixtures?date=2019-10-22\"); // Get next X available fixtures get(\"https://v3.football.api-sports.io/fixtures?next=15\"); // Get last X available fixtures get(\"https://v3.football.api-sports.io/fixtures?last=15\"); // It’s possible to make requests by mixing the available parameters get(\"https://v3.football.api-sports.io/fixtures?date=2020-01-30&league=61&season=2019\"); get(\"https://v3.football.api-sports.io/fixtures?league=61&next=10\"); get(\"https://v3.football.api-sports.io/fixtures?venue=358&next=10\"); get(\"https://v3.football.api-sports.io/fixtures?league=61&last=10&status=ft\");       - generic [ref=e4427]:
        - generic [ref=e4428]:
          - heading "tag/Fixtures/operation/get-fixtures-lineups Lineups" [level=2] [ref=e4429]:
            - link "tag/Fixtures/operation/get-fixtures-lineups" [ref=e4430] [cursor=pointer]:
              - /url: "#tag/Fixtures/operation/get-fixtures-lineups"
            - text: Lineups
          - generic [ref=e4432]:
            - paragraph [ref=e4433]: Get the lineups for a fixture.
            - paragraph [ref=e4434]:
              - text: Lineups are available between 20 and 40 minutes before the fixture when the competition covers this feature. You can check this with the endpoint
              - code [ref=e4435]: leagues
              - text: and the
              - code [ref=e4436]: coverage
              - text: field.
            - blockquote [ref=e4437]:
              - paragraph [ref=e4438]: It's possible that for some competitions the lineups are not available before the fixture, in this case, they are updated and available after the match with a variable delay depending on the competition.
            - paragraph [ref=e4439]:
              - strong [ref=e4440]: Available datas
            - list [ref=e4441]:
              - listitem [ref=e4442]: Formation
              - listitem [ref=e4443]: Coach
              - listitem [ref=e4444]: Start XI
              - listitem [ref=e4445]: Substitutes
            - paragraph [ref=e4446]:
              - strong [ref=e4447]:
                - text: Players' positions on the grid
                - code [ref=e4448]: "*"
            - paragraph [ref=e4449]:
              - strong [ref=e4450]: X
              - text: = row and
              - strong [ref=e4451]: "Y"
              - text: = column (X:Y)
            - paragraph [ref=e4452]:
              - text: Line 1
              - strong [ref=e4453]: X
              - text: being the one of the goal and then for each line this number is incremented. The column
              - strong [ref=e4454]: "Y"
              - text: will go from left to right, and incremented for each player of the line.
            - paragraph [ref=e4455]:
              - code [ref=e4456]: "* As a new feature, some irregularities may occur, do not hesitate to report them on our public Roadmap"
            - paragraph [ref=e4457]:
              - strong [ref=e4458]: Update Frequency
              - text: ": This endpoint is updated every 15 minutes."
            - paragraph [ref=e4459]:
              - strong [ref=e4460]: Recommended Calls
              - text: ": 1 call every 15 minutes for the fixtures in progress otherwise 1 call per day."
            - blockquote [ref=e4461]:
              - paragraph [ref=e4462]: Here are several examples of what can be done
            - paragraph [ref=e4463]:
              - img "demo-lineups" [ref=e4464]
            - paragraph [ref=e4465]:
              - img "demo-lineups" [ref=e4466]

        - text: Predictions
      - generic [ref=e5079]:
        - generic [ref=e5080]:
          - heading "tag/Predictions/operation/get-predictions Predictions" [level=2] [ref=e5081]:
            - link "tag/Predictions/operation/get-predictions" [ref=e5082] [cursor=pointer]:
              - /url: "#tag/Predictions/operation/get-predictions"
            - text: Predictions
          - generic [ref=e5084]:
            - paragraph [ref=e5085]: Get predictions about a fixture.
            - paragraph [ref=e5086]: The predictions are made using several algorithms including the poisson distribution, comparison of team statistics, last matches, players etc…
            - paragraph [ref=e5087]: Bookmakers odds are not used to make these predictions
            - paragraph [ref=e5088]: Also provides some comparative statistics between teams
            - paragraph [ref=e5089]:
              - strong [ref=e5090]: Available Predictions
            - list [ref=e5091]:
              - listitem [ref=e5092]: "Match winner : Id of the team that can potentially win the fixture"
              - listitem [ref=e5093]:
                - text: "Win or Draw : If"
                - code [ref=e5094]: "True"
                - text: indicates that the designated team can win or draw
              - listitem [ref=e5095]:
                - text: "Under / Over : -1.5 / -2.5 / -3.5 / -4.5 / +1.5 / +2.5 / +3.5 / +4.5"
                - code [ref=e5096]: "*"
              - listitem [ref=e5097]:
                - text: "Goals Home : -1.5 / -2.5 / -3.5 / -4.5"
                - code [ref=e5098]: "*"
              - listitem [ref=e5099]:
                - text: Goals Away -1.5 / -2.5 / -3.5 / -4.5
                - code [ref=e5100]: "*"
              - listitem [ref=e5101]:
                - text: Advice
                - emphasis [ref=e5102]: "(Ex : Deportivo Santani or draws and -3.5 goals)"
            - paragraph [ref=e5103]:
              - code [ref=e5104]: "*"
              - strong [ref=e5105]: "-1.5"
              - text: means that there will be a maximum of
              - strong [ref=e5106]: "1.5"
              - text: "goals in the fixture, i.e :"
              - strong [ref=e5107]: "1"
              - text: goal
            - paragraph [ref=e5108]:
              - strong [ref=e5109]: Update Frequency
              - text: ": This endpoint is updated every hour."
            - paragraph [ref=e5110]:
              - strong [ref=e5111]: Recommended Calls
              - text: ": 1 call per hour for the fixtures in progress otherwise 1 call per day."

      - generic [ref=e5812]:
        - generic [ref=e5813]:
          - heading "tag/Players/operation/get-players Statistics" [level=2] [ref=e5814]:
            - link "tag/Players/operation/get-players" [ref=e5815] [cursor=pointer]:
              - /url: "#tag/Players/operation/get-players"
            - text: Statistics
          - generic [ref=e5817]:
            - paragraph [ref=e5818]: Get players statistics.
            - paragraph [ref=e5819]:
              - text: This endpoint returns the players for whom the
              - code [ref=e5820]: profile
              - text: and
              - code [ref=e5821]: statistics
              - text: data are available. Note that it is possible that a player has statistics for 2 teams in the same season in case of transfers.
            - paragraph [ref=e5822]:
              - text: The statistics are calculated according to the team
              - code [ref=e5823]: id
              - text: ", league"
              - code [ref=e5824]: id
              - text: and
              - code [ref=e5825]: season
              - text: .
            - paragraph [ref=e5826]:
              - text: You can find the available
              - code [ref=e5827]: seasons
              - text: by using the endpoint
              - code [ref=e5828]: players/seasons
              - text: .
            - blockquote [ref=e5829]:
              - paragraph [ref=e5830]:
                - text: To get the squads of the teams it is better to use the endpoint
                - code [ref=e5831]: players/squads
                - text: .
            - paragraph [ref=e5832]:
              - text: The players
              - code [ref=e5833]: id
              - text: are unique in the API and players keep it among all the teams they have been in.
            - paragraph [ref=e5834]:
              - text: In this endpoint you have the
              - code [ref=e5835]: rating
              - text: field, which is the rating of the player according to a match or a season. This data is calculated according to the performance of the player in relation to the other players of the game or the season who occupy the same position
              - emphasis [ref=e5836]: (Attacker, defender, goal...)
              - text: . There are different algorithms that take into account the position of the player and assign points according to his performance.
            - paragraph [ref=e5837]:
              - text: "To get the photo of a player you have to call the following url:"
              - code [ref=e5838]: "https://media.api-sports.io/football/players/{player_id}.png"
            - paragraph [ref=e5839]:
              - text: This endpoint uses a
              - strong [ref=e5840]: pagination system
              - text: ", you can navigate between the different pages with to the"
              - code [ref=e5841]: page
              - text: parameter.
            - blockquote [ref=e5842]:
              - paragraph [ref=e5843]:
                - strong [ref=e5844]: Pagination
                - text: ": 20 results per page."
            - paragraph [ref=e5845]:
              - strong [ref=e5846]: Update Frequency
              - text: ": This endpoint is updated several times a week."
            - paragraph [ref=e5847]:
              - strong [ref=e5848]: Recommended Calls
              - text: ": 1 call per day."
            - paragraph [ref=e5849]:
              - strong [ref=e5850]: Tutorials
              - text: ":"

            - link "tag/Players/operation/get-players-topredcards" [ref=e7050] [cursor=pointer]:
              - /url: "#tag/Players/operation/get-players-topredcards"
            - text: Top Red Cards
          - generic [ref=e7052]:
            - paragraph [ref=e7053]: Get the 20 players with the most red cards for a league or cup.
            - paragraph [ref=e7054]:
              - strong [ref=e7055]: "How it is calculated:"
            - list [ref=e7056]:
              - listitem [ref=e7057]: "1 : The player that received the higher number of red cards"
              - listitem [ref=e7058]: "2 : The player that received the higher number of yellow cards"
              - listitem [ref=e7059]: "3 : The player that assists in the higher number of matches"
              - listitem [ref=e7060]: "4 : The player that played the fewer minutes"
            - paragraph [ref=e7061]:
              - strong [ref=e7062]: Update Frequency
              - text: ": This endpoint is updated several times a week."
            - paragraph [ref=e7063]:
              - strong [ref=e7064]: Recommended Calls
              - text: ": 1 call per day."

      - generic [ref=e7970]:
        - generic [ref=e7971]:
          - heading "tag/Odds-(In-Play)/operation/get-odds-live odds/live" [level=2] [ref=e7972]:
            - link "tag/Odds-(In-Play)/operation/get-odds-live" [ref=e7973] [cursor=pointer]:
              - /url: "#tag/Odds-(In-Play)/operation/get-odds-live"
            - text: odds/live
          - generic [ref=e7975]:
            - paragraph [ref=e7976]: This endpoint returns in-play odds for fixtures in progress.
            - paragraph [ref=e7977]:
              - text: Fixtures are added between 15 and 5 minutes before the start of the fixture. Once the fixture is over they are removed from the endpoint between 5 and 20 minutes.
              - strong [ref=e7978]: No history is stored
              - text: . So fixtures that are about to start, fixtures in progress and fixtures that have just ended are available in this endpoint.
            - paragraph [ref=e7979]:
              - strong [ref=e7980]: Update Frequency
              - text: ": This endpoint is updated every 5 seconds."
              - code [ref=e7981]: "*"
            - paragraph [ref=e7982]:
              - code [ref=e7983]: "* This value can change in the range of 5 to 60 seconds"
            - paragraph [ref=e7984]:
              - strong [ref=e7985]: INFORMATIONS ABOUT STATUS
            - code [ref=e7987]: "\"status\": { \"stopped\": false, // True if the fixture is stopped by the referee for X reason \"blocked\": false, // True if bets on this fixture are temporarily blocked \"finished\": false // True if the fixture has not started or if it is finished },"
            - paragraph [ref=e7988]:
              - strong [ref=e7989]: INFORMATIONS ABOUT VALUES
            - paragraph [ref=e7990]:
              - text: When several identical values exist for the same bet the
              - code [ref=e7991]: main
              - text: field is set to
              - code [ref=e7992]: "True"
              - text: for the bet being considered, the others will have the value
              - code [ref=e7993]: "False"
              - text: .
            - paragraph [ref=e7994]:
              - text: The
              - code [ref=e7995]: main
              - text: field will be set to
              - code [ref=e7996]: "True"
              - text: only if several identical values exist for the same bet.
            - paragraph [ref=e7997]:
              - text: When a value is unique for a bet the
              - code [ref=e7998]: main
              - text: value will always be
              - code [ref=e7999]: "False"
              - text: or
              - code [ref=e8000]: "null"
              - text: .
            - paragraph [ref=e8001]:
              - strong [ref=e8002]: Example below
              - text: ":"
            - code [ref=e8004]: "\"id\": 36, \"name\": \"Over/Under Line\", \"values\": [ { \"value\": \"Over\", \"odd\": \"1.975\", \"handicap\": \"2\", \"main\": true, // Bet to consider \"suspended\": false // True if this bet is temporarily suspended }, { \"value\": \"Over\", \"odd\": \"3.45\", \"handicap\": \"2\", \"main\": false, // Bet to no consider \"suspended\": false }, ]"

          - generic [ref=e8867]:
            - paragraph [ref=e8868]: Get odds from fixtures, leagues or date.
            - paragraph [ref=e8869]:
              - text: This endpoint uses a
              - strong [ref=e8870]: pagination system
              - text: ", you can navigate between the different pages with to the"
              - code [ref=e8871]: page
              - text: parameter.
            - blockquote [ref=e8872]:
              - paragraph [ref=e8873]:
                - strong [ref=e8874]: Pagination
                - text: ": 10 results per page."
            - paragraph [ref=e8875]: We provide pre-match odds between 1 and 14 days before the fixture.
            - paragraph [ref=e8876]:
              - text: We keep a 7-days history
              - emphasis [ref=e8877]: (The availability of odds may vary according to the leagues, seasons, fixtures and bookmakers)
            - paragraph [ref=e8878]:
              - strong [ref=e8879]: Update Frequency
              - text: ": This endpoint is updated every 3 hours."
            - paragraph [ref=e8880]:
              - strong [ref=e8881]: Recommended Calls
              - text: ": 1 call every 3 hours."
